pdateSummary(rows) {
      const model = currentModel();
      document.getElementById('totalValue').textContent = rows.length;
      const twse = rows.filter(row => row.market === '上市').length;
      const tpex = rows.filter(row => row.market === '上櫃').length;
      document.getElementById('marketValue').textContent = `${twse} / ${tpex}`;
      document.getElementById('volumeValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => row.volume).length;
      document.getElementById('strongValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => modelStrong(row, model)).length;
      document.getElementById('macdPositiveValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => modelMacdPositive(row, model)).length;
      dataDateEl.textContent = selectedDate;
      renderQuickList(rows);
    }
    function render() {
      syncModelLabels();
      const filtered = getFilteredRows();
      updateSummary(filtered);

      filtered.sort((a, b) => {
        const av = sortKey === 'strong' ? modelStrong(a)
          : sortKey === 'macdPositive' ? modelMacdPositive(a)
          : sortKey === 'model' ? modelLabel(a)
          : a[sortKey];
        const bv = sortKey === 'strong' ? modelStrong(b)
          : sortKey === 'macdPositive' ? modelMacdPositive(b)
          : sortKey === 'model' ? modelLabel(b)
          : b[sortKey];
        if (av === null || av === undefined) return bv === null || bv === undefined ? 0 : 1;
        if (bv === null || bv === undefined) return -1;
        let result;
        if (typeof av === 'number' && typeof bv === 'number') {
          result = av - bv;
        } else if (typeof av === 'boolean' && typeof bv === 'boolean') {
          result = Number(av) - Number(bv);
        } else {
          result = String(av).localeCompare(String(bv), 'zh-Hant', { numeric: true });
        }
        return sortDirection === 'asc' ? result : -result;
      });

      document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {

        th.classList.remove('asc', 'desc');
        if (th.dataset.sort === sortKey) th.classList.add(sortDirection);
      });

      countEl.textContent = `顯示 ${filtered.length} / ${DATA.length} 檔`;
      if (!filtered.length) {
        rowsEl.innerHTML = '<tr><td class="empty" colspan="15">沒有符合目前篩選條件的股票。</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      const relativeStrength = value => {
        if (value === null || value === undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(value))) {
          return '<span class="rs-neutral">—</span>';
        }
        const number = Number(value);
        const className = number > 0 ? 'rs-positive' : number < 0 ? 'rs-negative' : 'rs-neutral';
        const prefix = number > 0 ? '+' : '';
        return `<span class="${className}">${prefix}${number.toFixed(2)}%</span>`;
      };

      const averageVolume = value => {
        if (value === null || value === undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(value))) return '—';
        return Math.round(Number(value)).toLocaleString('zh-TW');
      };

      rowsEl.innerHTML = filtered.map(row => `
        <tr>
          <td data-label="市場">${row.market}</td>
          <td data-label="代號"><strong>${row.code}</strong></td>
          <td data-label="名稱">${row.name}</td>
          <td data-label="KD日期">${row.kd}</td>
          <td data-label="MACD日期">${row.macd}</td>
          <td data-label="量能">${badge(row.volume)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="20日平均成交張數">${averageVolume(row.avgVolume20)}</td>
          <td data-label="KD3強勢">${modelStrong(row) ? '<span class="badge strong">Y</span>' : 'N'}</td>
          <td data-label="符合模型">${modelLabel(row)}</td>
          <td data-label="MACD > 0">${badge(modelMacdPositive(row))}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="5日相對強度">${relativeStrength(row.rs5)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="20日相對強度">${relativeStrength(row.rs20)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="收盤價">${Number(row.close).toFixed(2)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="連續天數">${row.streak}</td>
          <td data-label="日K"><button class="chart-button" type="button" data-code="${row.code}">日K</button></td>
          <td class="mobile-chart-cell"><div class="mobile-chart" data-code="${row.code}"></div></td>
        </tr>
      `).join('');
      rowsEl.querySelectorAll('.mobile-chart').forEach(el => {
        const row = DATA.find(item => item.code === el.dataset.code);
        if (row) el.innerHTML = chartSvgMarkup(row, true);
      });
    }

    controls.forEach(el => el.addEventListener('input', render));
    controls.forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', render));
    reportDateEl.addEventListener('change', () => {
      selectedDate = reportDateEl.value;
      DATA = HISTORY[selectedDate] || [];
      chartPanel.hidden = true;
      render();
    });
    rowsEl.addEventListener('click', event => {
      const button = event.target.closest('.chart-button');
      if (!button) return;
      const row = DATA.find(item => item.code === button.dataset.code);
      if (row) renderChart(row);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {
      th.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const key = th.dataset.sort;
        if (sortKey === key) {
          sortDirection = sortDirection === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
        } else {
          sortKey = key;
          sortDirection = ['close', 'streak', 'volume', 'avgVolume20', 'strong', 'macdPositive', 'rs5', 'rs20'].includes(key) ? 'desc' : 'asc';
        }
        render();
      });
    });
    render();
  </script>

    const PASSWORD_HASH = "bd14620394b6083fbf735bbfc55773765de1d750ec053f281fe77d7764d7b36b";

            <li>KD3強勢：近期 KD 黃金交叉後，K-D 差距持續擴大。</li>
            <li><strong>相對強度（RS）：個股同期報酬率減去所屬市場指數同期報酬率。</strong>5 日約代表一週，20 日約代表一個月；上市股票與加權指數比較，上櫃股票與櫃買指數比較。正值表示同期跑贏大盤，負值表示同期落後大盤，僅供排序比較，不代表未來漲跌。</li>
          </ul>

pdateSummary(rows) {
      const model = currentModel();
      document.getElementById('totalValue').textContent = rows.length;
      const twse = rows.filter(row => row.market === '上市').length;
      const tpex = rows.filter(row => row.market === '上櫃').length;
      document.getElementById('marketValue').textContent = `${twse} / ${tpex}`;
      document.getElementById('volumeValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => row.volume).length;
      document.getElementById('strongValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => modelStrong(row, model)).length;
      document.getElementById('macdPositiveValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => modelMacdPositive(row, model)).length;
      dataDateEl.textContent = selectedDate;
      renderQuickList(rows);
    }
    function render() {
      syncModelLabels();
      const filtered = getFilteredRows();
      updateSummary(filtered);

      filtered.sort((a, b) => {
        const av = sortKey === 'strong' ? modelStrong(a)
          : sortKey === 'macdPositive' ? modelMacdPositive(a)
          : sortKey === 'model' ? modelLabel(a)
          : a[sortKey];
        const bv = sortKey === 'strong' ? modelStrong(b)
          : sortKey === 'macdPositive' ? modelMacdPositive(b)
          : sortKey === 'model' ? modelLabel(b)
          : b[sortKey];
        if (av === null || av === undefined) return bv === null || bv === undefined ? 0 : 1;
        if (bv === null || bv === undefined) return -1;
        let result;
        if (typeof av === 'number' && typeof bv === 'number') {
          result = av - bv;
        } else if (typeof av === 'boolean' && typeof bv === 'boolean') {
          result = Number(av) - Number(bv);
        } else {
          result = String(av).localeCompare(String(bv), 'zh-Hant', { numeric: true });
        }
        return sortDirection === 'asc' ? result : -result;
      });

      document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {

        th.classList.remove('asc', 'desc');
        if (th.dataset.sort === sortKey) th.classList.add(sortDirection);
      });

      countEl.textContent = `顯示 ${filtered.length} / ${DATA.length} 檔`;
      if (!filtered.length) {
        rowsEl.innerHTML = '<tr><td class="empty" colspan="15">沒有符合目前篩選條件的股票。</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      const relativeStrength = value => {
        if (value === null || value === undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(value))) {
          return '<span class="rs-neutral">—</span>';
        }
        const number = Number(value);
        const className = number > 0 ? 'rs-positive' : number < 0 ? 'rs-negative' : 'rs-neutral';
        const prefix = number > 0 ? '+' : '';
        return `<span class="${className}">${prefix}${number.toFixed(2)}%</span>`;
      };

      const averageVolume = value => {
        if (value === null || value === undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(value))) return '—';
        return Math.round(Number(value)).toLocaleString('zh-TW');
      };

      rowsEl.innerHTML = filtered.map(row => `
        <tr>
          <td data-label="市場">${row.market}</td>
          <td data-label="代號"><strong>${row.code}</strong></td>
          <td data-label="名稱">${row.name}</td>
          <td data-label="KD日期">${row.kd}</td>
          <td data-label="MACD日期">${row.macd}</td>
          <td data-label="量能">${badge(row.volume)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="20日平均成交張數">${averageVolume(row.avgVolume20)}</td>
          <td data-label="KD3強勢">${modelStrong(row) ? '<span class="badge strong">Y</span>' : 'N'}</td>
          <td data-label="符合模型">${modelLabel(row)}</td>
          <td data-label="MACD > 0">${badge(modelMacdPositive(row))}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="5日相對強度">${relativeStrength(row.rs5)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="20日相對強度">${relativeStrength(row.rs20)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="收盤價">${Number(row.close).toFixed(2)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="連續天數">${row.streak}</td>
          <td data-label="日K"><button class="chart-button" type="button" data-code="${row.code}">日K</button></td>
          <td class="mobile-chart-cell"><div class="mobile-chart" data-code="${row.code}"></div></td>
        </tr>
      `).join('');
      rowsEl.querySelectorAll('.mobile-chart').forEach(el => {
        const row = DATA.find(item => item.code === el.dataset.code);
        if (row) el.innerHTML = chartSvgMarkup(row, true);
      });
    }

    controls.forEach(el => el.addEventListener('input', render));
    controls.forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', render));
    reportDateEl.addEventListener('change', () => {
      selectedDate = reportDateEl.value;
      DATA = HISTORY[selectedDate] || [];
      chartPanel.hidden = true;
      render();
    });
    rowsEl.addEventListener('click', event => {
      const button = event.target.closest('.chart-button');
      if (!button) return;
      const row = DATA.find(item => item.code === button.dataset.code);
      if (row) renderChart(row);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {
      th.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const key = th.dataset.sort;
        if (sortKey === key) {
          sortDirection = sortDirection === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
        } else {
          sortKey = key;
          sortDirection = ['close', 'streak', 'volume', 'avgVolume20', 'strong', 'macdPositive', 'rs5', 'rs20'].includes(key) ? 'desc' : 'asc';
        }
        render();
      });
    });
    render();
  </script>
</body>

            <li>KD3強勢：近期 KD 黃金交叉後，K-D 差距持續擴大。</li>
            <li><strong>相對強度（RS）：個股同期報酬率減去所屬市場指數同期報酬率。</strong>5 日約代表一週，20 日約代表一個月；上市股票與加權指數比較，上櫃股票與櫃買指數比較。正值表示同期跑贏大盤，負值表示同期落後大盤，僅供排序比較，不代表未來漲跌。</li>
          </ul>

pdateSummary(rows) {
      const model = currentModel();
      document.getElementById('totalValue').textContent = rows.length;
      const twse = rows.filter(row => row.market === '上市').length;
      const tpex = rows.filter(row => row.market === '上櫃').length;
      document.getElementById('marketValue').textContent = `${twse} / ${tpex}`;
      document.getElementById('volumeValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => row.volume).length;
      document.getElementById('strongValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => modelStrong(row, model)).length;
      document.getElementById('macdPositiveValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => modelMacdPositive(row, model)).length;
      dataDateEl.textContent = selectedDate;
      renderQuickList(rows);
    }
    function render() {
      syncModelLabels();
      const filtered = getFilteredRows();
      updateSummary(filtered);

      filtered.sort((a, b) => {
        const av = sortKey === 'strong' ? modelStrong(a)
          : sortKey === 'macdPositive' ? modelMacdPositive(a)
          : sortKey === 'model' ? modelLabel(a)
          : a[sortKey];
        const bv = sortKey === 'strong' ? modelStrong(b)
          : sortKey === 'macdPositive' ? modelMacdPositive(b)
          : sortKey === 'model' ? modelLabel(b)
          : b[sortKey];
        if (av === null || av === undefined) return bv === null || bv === undefined ? 0 : 1;
        if (bv === null || bv === undefined) return -1;
        let result;
        if (typeof av === 'number' && typeof bv === 'number') {
          result = av - bv;
        } else if (typeof av === 'boolean' && typeof bv === 'boolean') {
          result = Number(av) - Number(bv);
        } else {
          result = String(av).localeCompare(String(bv), 'zh-Hant', { numeric: true });
        }
        return sortDirection === 'asc' ? result : -result;
      });

      document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {

        th.classList.remove('asc', 'desc');
        if (th.dataset.sort === sortKey) th.classList.add(sortDirection);
      });

      countEl.textContent = `顯示 ${filtered.length} / ${DATA.length} 檔`;
      if (!filtered.length) {
        rowsEl.innerHTML = '<tr><td class="empty" colspan="15">沒有符合目前篩選條件的股票。</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      const relativeStrength = value => {
        if (value === null || value === undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(value))) {
          return '<span class="rs-neutral">—</span>';
        }
        const number = Number(value);
        const className = number > 0 ? 'rs-positive' : number < 0 ? 'rs-negative' : 'rs-neutral';
        const prefix = number > 0 ? '+' : '';
        return `<span class="${className}">${prefix}${number.toFixed(2)}%</span>`;
      };

      const averageVolume = value => {
        if (value === null || value === undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(value))) return '—';
        return Math.round(Number(value)).toLocaleString('zh-TW');
      };

      rowsEl.innerHTML = filtered.map(row => `
        <tr>
          <td data-label="市場">${row.market}</td>
          <td data-label="代號"><strong>${row.code}</strong></td>
          <td data-label="名稱">${row.name}</td>
          <td data-label="KD日期">${row.kd}</td>
          <td data-label="MACD日期">${row.macd}</td>
          <td data-label="量能">${badge(row.volume)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="20日平均成交張數">${averageVolume(row.avgVolume20)}</td>
          <td data-label="KD3強勢">${modelStrong(row) ? '<span class="badge strong">Y</span>' : 'N'}</td>
          <td data-label="符合模型">${modelLabel(row)}</td>
          <td data-label="MACD > 0">${badge(modelMacdPositive(row))}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="5日相對強度">${relativeStrength(row.rs5)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="20日相對強度">${relativeStrength(row.rs20)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="收盤價">${Number(row.close).toFixed(2)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="連續天數">${row.streak}</td>
          <td data-label="日K"><button class="chart-button" type="button" data-code="${row.code}">日K</button></td>
          <td class="mobile-chart-cell"><div class="mobile-chart" data-code="${row.code}"></div></td>
        </tr>
      `).join('');
      rowsEl.querySelectorAll('.mobile-chart').forEach(el => {
        const row = DATA.find(item => item.code === el.dataset.code);
        if (row) el.innerHTML = chartSvgMarkup(row, true);
      });
    }

    controls.forEach(el => el.addEventListener('input', render));
    controls.forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', render));
    reportDateEl.addEventListener('change', () => {
      selectedDate = reportDateEl.value;
      DATA = HISTORY[selectedDate] || [];
      chartPanel.hidden = true;
      render();
    });
    rowsEl.addEventListener('click', event => {
      const button = event.target.closest('.chart-button');
      if (!button) return;
      const row = DATA.find(item => item.code === button.dataset.code);
      if (row) renderChart(row);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {
      th.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const key = th.dataset.sort;
        if (sortKey === key) {
          sortDirection = sortDirection === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
        } else {
          sortKey = key;
          sortDirection = ['close', 'streak', 'volume', 'avgVolume20', 'strong', 'macdPositive', 'rs5', 'rs20'].includes(key) ? 'desc' : 'asc';
        }
        render();
      });
    });
    render();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

          <h2>策略說明</h2>
          <ul>
            <li>KD：觀察短線動能，名單列出近期 KD 黃金交叉標的。</li>
            <li>MACD：可切換 MACD130 或 MACD60 篩選法，動能條件均使用最近 3 日 Hist 判斷。</li>
            <li>MACD > 0：目前所選模型的 MACD 訊號線為正；預設不作硬性排除，勾選後才篩選。</li>
            <li>量能：Y 表示 5 日均量大於 10 日均量。</li>
            <li>20日均量：最近 20 個交易資料日的平均成交張數；上市成交股數除以 1,000，上櫃成交仟股直接換算為張。篩選只影響畫面顯示，不改變原始選股名單。</li>
            <li>KD3強勢：近期 KD 黃金交叉後，K-D 差距持續擴大。</li>
            <li><strong>相對強度（RS）：個股同期報酬率減去所屬市場指數同期報酬率。</strong>5 日約代表一週，20 日約代表一個月；上市股票與加權指數比較，上櫃股票與櫃買指數比較。正值表示同期跑贏大盤，負值表示同期落後大盤，僅供排序比較，不代表未來漲跌。</li>
          </ul>
        </div>

    async function sha256(text) {
      const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text);
      const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', bytes);
      return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
    }

    function unlockReport() {
      document.body.classList.remove('locked');
      sessionStorage.setItem(`kdmacd_report_auth_${PASSWORD_DATE}`, '1');
    }

    async function checkPassword(value) {
      return await sha256(value) === PASSWORD_HASH;
    }

    const passwordForm = document.getElementById('passwordForm');
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('passwordInput');
    const passwordError = document.getElementById('passwordError');
    if (sessionStorage.getItem(`kdmacd_report_auth_${PASSWORD_DATE}`) === '1') {
      unlockReport();
    }
    passwordForm.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
      event.preventDefault();
      const value = passwordInput.value.trim();
      if (await checkPassword(value)) {
        unlockReport();
        passwordError.textContent = '';
      } else {
        passwordError.textContent = '密碼錯誤，請確認 LINE 推播中的今日密碼。';
        passwordInput.select();
      }
    });
    function badge(value) {
      return value
        ? '<span class="badge yes">Y</span>'
        : '<span class="badge no">N</span>';
    }
    function htmlEscape(value) {
      return String(value)
        .replaceAll('&', '&amp;')
        .replaceAll('<', '&lt;')
        .replaceAll('>', '&gt;')
        .replaceAll('"', '&quot;')
        .replaceAll("'", '&#39;');
    }
    function currentModel() {
      return document.getElementById('macdModel').value;
    }
    function modelSignal(row, model = currentModel()) {
      return model === '60' ? row.signal60 : row.signal130;
    }
    function modelStrong(row, model = currentModel()) {
      return model === '60' ? row.strong60 : row.strong130;
    }
    function modelMacdPositive(row, model = currentModel()) {
      return model === '60' ? row.macd60 : row.macd130;
    }
    function modelLabel(row) {
      const labels = [];
      if (row.signal130) labels.push('130');
      if (row.signal60) labels.push('60');
      return labels.join('/') || '—';
    }
    function syncModelLabels() {
      const model = currentModel();
      document.getElementById('macdPositiveOnlyText').textContent = `只看 MACD${model} > 0`;
      document.getElementById('macdPositiveStatLabel').textContent = `MACD${model} > 0`;
    }
    function getFilteredRows() {
      const q = document.getElementById('search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
      const model = currentModel();
      const market = document.getElementById('market').value;
      const minAvgVolume = Number(document.getElementById('minAvgVolume').value);
      const volumeOnly = document.getElementById('volumeOnly').checked;
      const strongOnly = document.getElementById('strongOnly').checked;
      const macdPositiveOnly = document.getElementById('macdPositiveOnly').checked;
      const minStreak = Number(document.getElementById('minStreak').value);

      return DATA.filter(row => {
        const signalOk = modelSignal(row, model);
        const keywordOk = !q || row.code.includes(q) || row.name.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const marketOk = !market || row.market === market;
        const avgVolumeOk = minAvgVolume <= 0 || (row.avgVolume20 !== null && row.avgVolume20 >= minAvgVolume);
        const volumeOk = !volumeOnly || row.volume;
        const strongOk = !strongOnly || modelStrong(row, model);
        const macdPositiveOk = !macdPositiveOnly || modelMacdPositive(row, model);
        const streakOk = row.streak >= minStreak;
        return signalOk && keywordOk && marketOk && avgVolumeOk && volumeOk && strongOk && macdPositiveOk && streakOk;
      });
    }
    function renderQuickList(rows) {
      quickListCountEl.textContent = `${rows.length} / ${DATA.length} 檔`;
      if (!rows.length) {
        quickListEl.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">這個日期沒有選股資料。</div>';
        return;
      }
      quickListEl.innerHTML = rows
        .map(row => `<span class="quick-chip"><span>${htmlEscape(row.name)}</span><code>${htmlEscape(row.code)}</code></span>`)
        .join('');
    }
    function chartPoints(row) {
      const key = `${row.market}:${row.code}`;
      return (CHARTS[key] || [])
        .filter(point => point[0] <= selectedDate)
        .slice(-80)
        .map(point => ({
          date: point[0],
          d: point[0].slice(5).replace('-', '/'),
          o: point[1], h: point[2], l: point[3], c: point[4], v: point[5]
        }));
    }

    async function sha256(text) {
      const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text);
      const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', bytes);
      return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
    }

    function unlockReport() {
      document.body.classList.remove('locked');
      sessionStorage.setItem(`kdmacd_report_auth_${PASSWORD_DATE}`, '1');
    }

    async function checkPassword(value) {
      return await sha256(value) === PASSWORD_HASH;
    }

    const passwordForm = document.getElementById('passwordForm');
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('passwordInput');
    const passwordError = document.getElementById('passwordError');
    if (sessionStorage.getItem(`kdmacd_report_auth_${PASSWORD_DATE}`) === '1') {
      unlockReport();
    }
    passwordForm.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
      event.preventDefault();
      const value = passwordInput.value.trim();
      if (await checkPassword(value)) {
        unlockReport();
        passwordError.textContent = '';
      } else {
        passwordError.textContent = '密碼錯誤，請確認 LINE 推播中的今日密碼。';
        passwordInput.select();
      }
    });
    function badge(value) {
      return value
        ? '<span class="badge yes">Y</span>'
        : '<span class="badge no">N</span>';
    }
    function htmlEscape(value) {
      return String(value)
        .replaceAll('&', '&amp;')
        .replaceAll('<', '&lt;')
        .replaceAll('>', '&gt;')
        .replaceAll('"', '&quot;')
        .replaceAll("'", '&#39;');
    }
    function currentModel() {
      return document.getElementById('macdModel').value;
    }
    function modelSignal(row, model = currentModel()) {
      return model === '60' ? row.signal60 : row.signal130;
    }
    function modelStrong(row, model = currentModel()) {
      return model === '60' ? row.strong60 : row.strong130;
    }
    function modelMacdPositive(row, model = currentModel()) {
      return model === '60' ? row.macd60 : row.macd130;
    }
    function modelLabel(row) {
      const labels = [];
      if (row.signal130) labels.push('130');
      if (row.signal60) labels.push('60');
      return labels.join('/') || '—';
    }
    function syncModelLabels() {
      const model = currentModel();
      document.getElementById('macdPositiveOnlyText').textContent = `只看 MACD${model} > 0`;
      document.getElementById('macdPositiveStatLabel').textContent = `MACD${model} > 0`;
    }
    function getFilteredRows() {
      const q = document.getElementById('search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
      const model = currentModel();
      const market = document.getElementById('market').value;
      const minAvgVolume = Number(document.getElementById('minAvgVolume').value);
      const volumeOnly = document.getElementById('volumeOnly').checked;
      const strongOnly = document.getElementById('strongOnly').checked;
      const macdPositiveOnly = document.getElementById('macdPositiveOnly').checked;
      const minStreak = Number(document.getElementById('minStreak').value);

      return DATA.filter(row => {
        const signalOk = modelSignal(row, model);
        const keywordOk = !q || row.code.includes(q) || row.name.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const marketOk = !market || row.market === market;
        const avgVolumeOk = minAvgVolume <= 0 || (row.avgVolume20 !== null && row.avgVolume20 >= minAvgVolume);
        const volumeOk = !volumeOnly || row.volume;
        const strongOk = !strongOnly || modelStrong(row, model);
        const macdPositiveOk = !macdPositiveOnly || modelMacdPositive(row, model);
        const streakOk = row.streak >= minStreak;
        return signalOk && keywordOk && marketOk && avgVolumeOk && volumeOk && strongOk && macdPositiveOk && streakOk;
      });
    }
    function renderQuickList(rows) {
      quickListCountEl.textContent = `${rows.length} / ${DATA.length} 檔`;
      if (!rows.length) {
        quickListEl.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">這個日期沒有選股資料。</div>';
        return;
      }
      quickListEl.innerHTML = rows
        .map(row => `<span class="quick-chip"><span>${htmlEscape(row.name)}</span><code>${htmlEscape(row.code)}</code></span>`)
        .join('');
    }
    function chartPoints(row) {
      const key = `${row.market}:${row.code}`;
      return (CHARTS[key] || [])
        .filter(point => point[0] <= selectedDate)
        .slice(-80)
        .map(point => ({
          date: point[0],
          d: point[0].slice(5).replace('-', '/'),
          o: point[1], h: point[2], l: point[3], c: point[4], v: point[5]
        }));
    }
    function chartSvgMarkup(row, compact = false) {
      const points = chartPoints(row);
      const width = compact ? 640 : 760;
      const height = compact ? 250 : 360;
      if (!points.length) {
        return `<svg class="inline-k-chart" viewBox="0 0 ${width} ${height}" role="img" aria-label="日 K 圖"><text x="${width / 2}" y="${height / 2}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#667085">沒有可用日 K 資料</text></svg>`;
      }

      const left = compact ? 44 : 54;
      const right = compact ? 12 : 18;
      const top = compact ? 14 : 18;
      const priceHeight = compact ? 150 : 238;
      const volumeTop = compact ? 184 : 278;
      const volumeHeight = compact ? 36 : 52;
      const labelY = compact ? 236 : 348;
      const maxHigh = Math.max(...points.map(p => p.h));
      const minLow = Math.min(...points.map(p => p.l));
      const pricePad = Math.max((maxHigh - minLow) * 0.06, maxHigh * 0.01, 1);
      const maxPricePad = maxHigh + pricePad;
      const minPricePad = minLow - pricePad;
      const scale = priceHeight / Math.max(maxPricePad - minPricePad, 1

    async function sha256(text) {
      const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text);
      const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', bytes);
      return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
    }

    function unlockReport() {
      document.body.classList.remove('locked');
      sessionStorage.setItem(`kdmacd_report_auth_${PASSWORD_DATE}`, '1');
    }

    async function checkPassword(value) {
      return await sha256(value) === PASSWORD_HASH;
    }

    const passwordForm = document.getElementById('passwordForm');
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('passwordInput');

    passwordForm.addEventListener('submit', async event => {

    function badge(value) {
      return value
        ? '<span class="badge yes">Y</span>'
        : '<span class="badge no">N</span>';
    }
    function htmlEscape(value) {
      return String(value)
        .replaceAll('&', '&amp;')
        .replaceAll('<', '&lt;')
        .replaceAll('>', '&gt;')
        .replaceAll('"', '&quot;')
        .replaceAll("'", '&#39;');
    }
    function currentModel() {
      return document.getElementById('macdModel').value;
    }
    function modelSignal(row, model = currentModel()) {
      return model === '60' ? row.signal60 : row.signal130;
    }
    function modelStrong(row, model = currentModel()) {
      return model === '60' ? row.strong60 : row.strong130;
    }
    function modelMacdPositive(row, model = currentModel()) {
      return model === '60' ? row.macd60 : row.macd130;
    }
    function modelLabel(row) {
      const labels = [];
      if (row.signal130) labels.push('130');
      if (row.signal60) labels.push('60');
      return labels.join('/') || '—';
    }
    function syncModelLabels() {
      const model = currentModel();
      document.getElementById('macdPositiveOnlyText').textContent = `只看 MACD${model} > 0`;
      document.getElementById('macdPositiveStatLabel').textContent = `MACD${model} > 0`;
    }
    function getFilteredRows() {
      const q = document.getElementById('search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
      const model = currentModel();
      const market = document.getElementById('market').value;
      const minAvgVolume = Number(document.getElementById('minAvgVolume').value);
      const volumeOnly = document.getElementById('volumeOnly').checked;
      const strongOnly = document.getElementById('strongOnly').checked;
      const macdPositiveOnly = document.getElementById('macdPositiveOnly').checked;
      const minStreak = Number(document.getElementById('minStreak').value);

      return DATA.filter(row => {
        const signalOk = modelSignal(row, model);
        const keywordOk = !q || row.code.includes(q) || row.name.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const marketOk = !market || row.market === market;
        const avgVolumeOk = minAvgVolume <= 0 || (row.avgVolume20 !== null && row.avgVolume20 >= minAvgVolume);
        const volumeOk = !volumeOnly || row.volume;
        const strongOk = !strongOnly || modelStrong(row, model);
        const macdPositiveOk = !macdPositiveOnly || modelMacdPositive(row, model);
        const streakOk = row.streak >= minStreak;
        return signalOk && keywordOk && marketOk && avgVolumeOk && volumeOk && strongOk && macdPositiveOk && streakOk;
      });
    }
    function renderQuickList(rows) {
      quickListCountEl.textContent = `${rows.length} / ${DATA.length} 檔`;
      if (!rows.length) {
        quickListEl.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">這個日期沒有選股資料。</div>';
        return;
      }
      quickListEl.innerHTML = rows
        .map(row => `<span class="quick-chip"><span>${htmlEscape(row.name)}</span><code>${htmlEscape(row.code)}</code></span>`)
        .join('');
    }

        }));
    }

    function chartPoints(row) {
      const key = `${row.market}:${row.code}`;

    function chartSvgMarkup(row, compact = false) {
      const points = chartPoints(row);
      const width = compact ? 640 : 760;
      const height = compact ? 250 : 360;
      if (!points.length) {
        return `<svg class="inline-k-chart" viewBox="0 0 ${width} ${height}" role="img" aria-label="日 K 圖"><text x="${width / 2}" y="${height / 2}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#667085">沒有可用日 K 資料</text></svg>`;
      }

      const left = compact ? 44 : 54;
      const right = compact ? 12 : 18;
      const top = compact ? 14 : 18;
      const priceHeight = compact ? 150 : 238;
      const volumeTop = compact ? 184 : 278;
      const volumeHeight = compact ? 36 : 52;
      const labelY = compact ? 236 : 348;
      const maxHigh = Math.max(...points.map(p => p.h));
      const minLow = Math.min(...points.map(p => p.l));
      const pricePad = Math.max((maxHigh - minLow) * 0.06, maxHigh * 0.01, 1);
      const maxPricePad = maxHigh + pricePad;
      const minPricePad = minLow - pricePad;
      const scale = priceHeight / Math.max(maxPricePad - minPricePad, 1);
      const maxVol = Math.max(...points.map(p => p.v || 0), 1);
      const slot = (width - left - right) / points.length;
      const bodyWidth = Math.max(Math.min(slot * 0.58, compact ? 6 : 8), compact ? 2 : 3);
      const gridValues = [0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1].map(r => maxPricePad - (maxPricePad - minPricePad) * r);

      const grid = gridValues.map(value => {
        const y = top + (maxPricePad - value) * scale;
        return `<line x1="${left}" y1="${y.toFixed(1)}" x2="${width - right}" y2="${y.toFixed(1)}" stroke="#e5e7eb"/>` +
          `<text x="${left - 8}" y="${(y + 4).toFixed(1)}" text-anchor="end" fill="#667085" font-size="${compact ? 10 : 11}">${value.toFixed(1)}</text>`;
      }).join('');

      const candles = points.map((p, i) => {
        const x = left + slot * i + slot / 2;
        const highY = top + (maxPricePad - p.h) * scale;
        const lowY = top + (maxPricePad - p.l) * scale;
        const openY = top + (maxPricePad - p.o) * scale;
        const closeY = top + (maxPricePad - p.c) * scale;
        const up = p.c >= p.o;
        const color = up ? '#dc2626' : '#16a34a';
        const rectY = Math.min(openY, closeY);
        const rectH = Math.max(Math.abs(openY - closeY), compact ? 1.2 : 1.5);
        const volH = Math.max((p.v || 0) / maxVol * volumeHeight, 1);
        const labelEvery = compact ? Math.ceil(points.length / 4) : Math.ceil(points.length / 6);
        const label = i % labelEvery === 0 ? `<text x="${x.toFixed(1)}" y="${labelY}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#667085" font-size="${compact ? 9 : 10}">${p.d}</text>` : '';
        return `<line x1="${x.toFixed(1)}" y1="${highY.toFixed(1)}" x2="${x.toFixed(1)}" y2="${lowY.toFixed(1)}" stroke="${color}" stroke-width="1.2"/>` +
          `<rect x="${(x - bodyWidth / 2).toFixed(1)}" y="${rectY.toFixed(1)}" width="${bodyWidth.toFixed(1)}" height="${rectH.toFixed(1)}" fill="${color}" opacity="0.9"/>` +
          `<rect x="${(x - bodyWidth / 2).toFixed(1)}" y="${(volumeTop + volumeHeight - volH).toFixed(1)}" width="${bodyWidth.toFixed(1)}" height="${volH.toFixed(1)}" fill="${color}" opacity="0.22"/>` + label;
      }).join('');

      const last = points[points.length - 1];
      return `<svg class="inline-k-chart" viewBox="0 0 ${width} ${height}" role="img" aria-label="${row.code} 日 K 圖">
        <rect x="0" y="0" width="${width}" height="${height}" fill="#fff"/>
        ${grid}
        <line x1="${left}" y1="${top}" x2="${left}" y2="${top + priceHeight}" stroke="#cbd5e1"/>
        <line x1="${left}" y1="${top + priceHeight}" x2="${width - right}" y2="${top + priceHeight}" stroke="#cbd5e1"/>
        <text x="${left}" y="${volumeTop - 8}" fill="#667085" font-size="${compact ? 10 : 11}">成交量</text>
        ${candles}
        <text x="${width - right}" y="18" text-anchor="end" fill="#18202f" font-size="${compact ? 11 : 12}" font-weight="700">收 ${Number(last.c).toFixed(2)}</text>
      </svg>`;
    }

    function renderChart(row) {
      chartPanel.hidden = false;
      chartTitle.textContent = `${row.code} ${row.name} 日 K 圖`;
      chartBox.innerHTML = chartSvgMarkup(row, false).replace('class="inline-k-chart"', 'id="kChart" class="inline-k-chart"');
      chartPanel.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
    }
    function updateSummary(rows) {
      const model = currentModel();
      document.getElementById('totalValue').textContent = rows.length;
      const twse = rows.filter(row => row.market === '上市').length;
      const tpex = rows.filter(row => row.market === '上櫃').length;
      document.getElementById('marketValue').textContent = `${twse} / ${tpex}`;
      document.getElementById('volumeValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => row.volume).length;
      document.getElementById('strongValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => modelStrong(row, model)).length;
      document.getElementById('macdPositiveValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => modelMacdPositive(row, model)).length;
      dataDateEl.textContent = selectedDate;
      renderQuickList(rows);
    }
    function render() {
      syncModelLabels();
      const filtered = getFilteredRows();
      updateSummary(filtered);

      filtered.sort((a, b) => {
        const av = sortKey === 'strong' ? modelStrong(a)
          : sortKey === 'macdPositive' ? modelMacdPositive(a)
          : sortKey === 'model' ? modelLabel(a)
          : a[sortKey];
        const bv = sortKey === 'strong' ? modelStrong(b)
          : sortKey === 'macdPositive' ? modelMacdPositive(b)
          : sortKey === 'model' ? modelLabel(b)

    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('passwordInput');
    const passwordError = document.getElementById('passwordError');
    if (sessionStorage.getItem(`kdmacd_report_auth_${PASSWORD_DATE}`) === '1') {
      unlockReport();
    }
    passwordForm.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
      event.preventDefault();
      const value = passwordInput.value.trim();
      if (await checkPassword(value)) {
        unlockReport();
        passwordError.textContent = '';
      } else {
        passwordError.textContent = '密碼錯誤，請確認 LINE 推播中的今日密碼。';
        passwordInput.select();
      }
    });
    function badge(value) {
      return value
        ? '<span class="badge yes">Y</span>'
        : '<span class="badge no">N</span>';
    }
    function htmlEscape(value) {
      return String(value)
        .replaceAll('&', '&amp;')
        .replaceAll('<', '&lt;')
        .replaceAll('>', '&gt;')
        .replaceAll('"', '&quot;')
        .replaceAll("'", '&#39;');
    }
    function currentModel() {
      return document.getElementById('macdModel').value;
    }
    function modelSignal(row, model = currentModel()) {
      return model === '60' ? row.signal60 : row.signal130;
    }
    function modelStrong(row, model = currentModel()) {
      return model === '60' ? row.strong60 : row.strong130;
    }
    function modelMacdPositive(row, model = currentModel()) {
      return model === '60' ? row.macd60 : row.macd130;
    }
    function modelLabel(row) {
      const labels = [];
      if (row.signal130) labels.push('130');
      if (row.signal60) labels.push('60');
      return labels.join('/') || '—';
    }
    function syncModelLabels() {
      const model = currentModel();
      document.getElementById('macdPositiveOnlyText').textContent = `只看 MACD${model} > 0`;
      document.getElementById('macdPositiveStatLabel').textContent = `MACD${model} > 0`;
    }
    function getFilteredRows() {
      const q = document.getElementById('search').value.trim().toLowerCase();
      const model = currentModel();
      const market = document.getElementById('market').value;
      const minAvgVolume = Number(document.getElementById('minAvgVolume').value);
      const volumeOnly = document.getElementById('volumeOnly').checked;
      const strongOnly = document.getElementById('strongOnly').checked;
      const macdPositiveOnly = document.getElementById('macdPositiveOnly').checked;
      const minStreak = Number(document.getElementById('minStreak').value);

      return DATA.filter(row => {
        const signalOk = modelSignal(row, model);
        const keywordOk = !q || row.code.includes(q) || row.name.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const marketOk = !market || row.market === market;
        const avgVolumeOk = minAvgVolume <= 0 || (row.avgVolume20 !== null && row.avgVolume20 >= minAvgVolume);
        const volumeOk = !volumeOnly || row.volume;
        const strongOk = !strongOnly || modelStrong(row, model);
        const macdPositiveOk = !macdPositiveOnly || modelMacdPositive(row, model);
        const streakOk = row.streak >= minStreak;
        return signalOk && keywordOk && marketOk && avgVolumeOk && volumeOk && strongOk && macdPositiveOk && streakOk;
      });
    }
    function renderQuickList(rows) {
      quickListCountEl.textContent = `${rows.length} / ${DATA.length} 檔`;
      if (!rows.length) {
        quickListEl.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">這個日期沒有選股資料。</div>';
        return;
      }
      quickListEl.innerHTML = rows
        .map(row => `<span class="quick-chip"><span>${htmlEscape(row.name)}</span><code>${htmlEscape(row.code)}</code></span>`)
        .join('');
    }
    function chartPoints(row) {
      const key = `${row.market}:${row.code}`;
      return (CHARTS[key] || [])
        .filter(point => point[0] <= selectedDate)
        .slice(-80)
        .map(point => ({
          date: point[0],
          d: point[0].slice(5).replace('-', '/'),
          o: point[1], h: point[2], l: point[3], c: point[4], v: point[5]
        }));
    }
    function chartSvgMarkup(row, compact = false) {
      const points = chartPoints(row);
      const width = compact ? 640 : 760;
      const height = compact ? 250 : 360;
      if (!points.length) {
        return `<svg class="inline-k-chart" viewBox="0 0 ${width} ${height}" role="img" aria-label="日 K 圖"><text x="${width / 2}" y="${height / 2}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#667085">沒有可用日 K 資料</text></svg>`;
      }

      const left = compact ? 44 : 54;
      const right = compact ? 12 : 18;
      const top = compact ? 14 : 18;
      const priceHeight = compact ? 150 : 238;
      const volumeTop = compact ? 184 : 278;
      const volumeHeight = compact ? 36 : 52;
      const labelY = compact ? 236 : 348;
      const maxHigh = Math.max(...points.map(p => p.h));
      const minLow = Math.min(...points.map(p => p.l));
      const pricePad = Math.max((maxHigh - minLow) * 0.06, maxHigh * 0.01, 1);
      const maxPricePad = maxHigh + pricePad;
      const minPricePad = minLow - pricePad;
      const scale = priceHeight / Math.max(maxPricePad - minPricePad, 1);
      const maxVol = Math.max(...points.map(p => p.v || 0), 1);
      const slot = (width - left - right) / points.length;
      const bodyWidth = Math.max(Math.min(slot * 0.58, compact ? 6 : 8), compact ? 2 : 3);
      const gridValues = [0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1].map(r => maxPricePad - (maxPricePad - minPricePad) * r);

      const grid = gridValues.map(value => {
        const y = top + (maxPricePad - value) * scale;
        return `<line x1="${left}" y1="${y.toFixed(1)}" x2="${width - right}" y2="${y.toFixed(1)}" stroke="#e5e7eb"/>` +
          `<text x="${left - 8}" y="${(y + 4).toFixed(1)}" text-anchor="end" fill="#667085" font-size="${compact ? 10 : 11}">${value.toFixed(1)}</text>`;
      }).join('');

      const candles = points.map((p, i) => {
        const x = left + slot * i + slot / 2;
        const highY = top + (maxPricePad - p.h) * scale;
        const lowY = top + (maxPricePad - p.l) * scale;
        const openY = top + (maxPricePad - p.o) * scale;
        const closeY = top + (maxPricePad - p.c) * scale;
        const up = p.c >= p.o;
        const color = up ? '#dc2626' : '#16a34a';
        const rectY = Math.min(openY, closeY);
        const rectH = Math.max(Math.abs(openY - closeY), compact ? 1.2 : 1.5);
        const volH = Math.max((p.v || 0) / maxVol * volumeHeight, 1);
        const labelEvery = compact ? Math.ceil(points.length / 4) : Math.ceil(points.length / 6);
        const label = i % labelEvery === 0 ? `<text x="${x.toFixed(1)}" y="${labelY}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#667085" font-size="${compact ? 9 : 10}">${p.d}</text>` : '';
        return `<line x1="${x.toFixed(1)}" y1="${highY.toFixed(1)}" x2="${x.toFixed(1)}" y2="${lowY.toFixed(1)}" stroke="${color}" stroke-width="1.2"/>` +
          `<rect x="${(x - bodyWidth / 2).toFixed(1)}" y="${rectY.toFixed(1)}" width="${bodyWidth.toFixed(1)}" height="${rectH.toFixed(1)}" fill="${color}" opacity="0.9"/>` +
          `<rect x="${(x - bodyWidth / 2).toFixed(1)}" y="${(volumeTop + volumeHeight - volH).toFixed(1)}" width="${bodyWidth.toFixed(1)}" height="${volH.toFixed(1)}" fill="${color}" opacity="0.22"/>` + label;
      }).join('');

      const last = points[points.length - 1];
      return `<svg class="inline-k-chart" viewBox="0 0 ${width} ${height}" role="img" aria-label="${row.code} 日 K 圖">
        <rect x="0" y="0" width="${width}" height="${height}" fill="#fff"/>
        ${grid}
        <line x1="${left}" y1="${top}" x2="${left}" y2="${top + priceHeight}" stroke="#cbd5e1"/>
        <line x1="${left}" y1="${top + priceHeight}" x2="${width - right}" y2="${top + priceHeight}" stroke="#cbd5e1"/>
        <text x="${left}" y="${volumeTop - 8}" fill="#667085" font-size="${compact ? 10 : 11}">成交量</text>
        ${candles}
        <text x="${width - right}" y="18" text-anchor="end" fill="#18202f" font-size="${compact ? 11 : 12}" font-weight="700">收 ${Number(last.c).toFixed(2)}</text>
      </svg>`;
    }

    function renderChart(row) {
      chartPanel.hidden = false;
      chartTitle.textContent = `${row.code} ${row.name} 日 K 圖`;
      chartBox.innerHTML = chartSvgMarkup(row, false).replace('class="inline-k-chart"', 'id="kChart" class="inline-k-chart"');
      chartPanel.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
    }
    function updateSummary(rows) {
      const model = currentModel();
      document.getElementById('totalValue').textContent = rows.length;
      const twse = rows.filter(row => row.market === '上市').length;
      const tpex = rows.filter(row => row.market === '上櫃').length;
      document.getElementById('marketValue').textContent = `${twse} / ${tpex}`;
      document.getElementById('volumeValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => row.volume).length;
      document.getElementById('strongValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => modelStrong(row, model)).length;
      document.getElementById('macdPositiveValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => modelMacdPositive(row, model)).length;
      dataDateEl.textContent = selectedDate;
      renderQuickList(rows);
    }
    function render() {
      syncModelLabels();
      const filtered = getFilteredRows();
      updateSummary(filtered);

      filtered.sort((a, b) => {
        const av = sortKey === 'strong' ? modelStrong(a)
          : sortKey === 'macdPositive' ? modelMacdPositive(a)
          : sortKey === 'model' ? modelLabel(a)
          : a[sortKey];
        const bv = sortKey === 'strong' ? modelStrong(b)
          : sortKey === 'macdPositive' ? modelMacdPositive(b)
          : sortKey === 'model' ? modelLabel(b)
          : b[sortKey];

      const minLow = Math.min(...points.map(p => p.l));
      const pricePad = Math.max((maxHigh - minLow) * 0.06, maxHigh * 0.01, 1);
      const maxPricePad = maxHigh + pricePad;
      const minPricePad = minLow - pricePad;
      const scale = priceHeight / Math.max(maxPricePad - minPricePad, 1);
      const maxVol = Math.max(...points.map(p => p.v || 0), 1);
      const slot = (width - left - right) / points.length;
      const bodyWidth = Math.max(Math.min(slot * 0.58, compact ? 6 : 8), compact ? 2 : 3);
      const gridValues = [0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1].map(r => maxPricePad - (maxPricePad - minPricePad) * r);

      const grid = gridValues.map(value => {
        const y = top + (maxPricePad - value) * scale;
        return `<line x1="${left}" y1="${y.toFixed(1)}" x2="${width - right}" y2="${y.toFixed(1)}" stroke="#e5e7eb"/>` +
          `<text x="${left - 8}" y="${(y + 4).toFixed(1)}" text-anchor="end" fill="#667085" font-size="${compact ? 10 : 11}">${value.toFixed(1)}</text>`;
      }).join('');

      const candles = points.map((p, i) => {
        const x = left + slot * i + slot / 2;
        const highY = top + (maxPricePad - p.h) * scale;
        const lowY = top + (maxPricePad - p.l) * scale;
        const openY = top + (maxPricePad - p.o) * scale;
        const closeY = top + (maxPricePad - p.c) * scale;
        const up = p.c >= p.o;
        const color = up ? '#dc2626' : '#16a34a';
        const rectY = Math.min(openY, closeY);
        const rectH = Math.max(Math.abs(openY - closeY), compact ? 1.2 : 1.5);
        const volH = Math.max((p.v || 0) / maxVol * volumeHeight, 1);
        const labelEvery = compact ? Math.ceil(points.length / 4) : Math.ceil(points.length / 6);
        const label = i % labelEvery === 0 ? `<text x="${x.toFixed(1)}" y="${labelY}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#667085" font-size="${compact ? 9 : 10}">${p.d}</text>` : '';
        return `<line x1="${x.toFixed(1)}" y1="${highY.toFixed(1)}" x2="${x.toFixed(1)}" y2="${lowY.toFixed(1)}" stroke="${color}" stroke-width="1.2"/>` +
          `<rect x="${(x - bodyWidth / 2).toFixed(1)}" y="${rectY.toFixed(1)}" width="${bodyWidth.toFixed(1)}" height="${rectH.toFixed(1)}" fill="${color}" opacity="0.9"/>` +
          `<rect x="${(x - bodyWidth / 2).toFixed(1)}" y="${(volumeTop + volumeHeight - volH).toFixed(1)}" width="${bodyWidth.toFixed(1)}" height="${volH.toFixed(1)}" fill="${color}" opacity="0.22"/>` + label;
      }).join('');

      const last = points[points.length - 1];
      return `<svg class="inline-k-chart" viewBox="0 0 ${width} ${height}" role="img" aria-label="${row.code} 日 K 圖">
        <rect x="0" y="0" width="${width}" height="${height}" fill="#fff"/>
        ${grid}
        <line x1="${left}" y1="${top}" x2="${left}" y2="${top + priceHeight}" stroke="#cbd5e1"/>
        <line x1="${left}" y1="${top + priceHeight}" x2="${width - right}" y2="${top + priceHeight}" stroke="#cbd5e1"/>
        <text x="${left}" y="${volumeTop - 8}" fill="#667085" font-size="${compact ? 10 : 11}">成交量</text>
        ${candles}
        <text x="${width - right}" y="18" text-anchor="end" fill="#18202f" font-size="${compact ? 11 : 12}" font-weight="700">收 ${Number(last.c).toFixed(2)}</text>
      </svg>`;
    }

    function renderChart(row) {
      chartPanel.hidden = false;
      chartTitle.textContent = `${row.code} ${row.name} 日 K 圖`;
      chartBox.innerHTML = chartSvgMarkup(row, false).replace('class="inline-k-chart"', 'id="kChart" class="inline-k-chart"');
      chartPanel.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
    }

      document.getElementById('totalValue').textContent = rows.length;

      const tpex = rows.filter(row => row.market === '上櫃').length;
      document.getElementById('marketValue').textContent = `${twse} / ${tpex}`;

      document.getElementById('macdPositiveValue').textContent = rows.filter(row => modelMacdPositive(row, model)).length;

    }

      syncModelLabels();

      filtered.sort((a, b) => {

          : b[sortKey];
        if (av === null || av === undefined) return bv === null || bv === undefined ? 0 : 1;
        if (bv === null || bv === undefined) return -1;
        let result;
        if (typeof av === 'n

    function updateSummary(rows) {

      const tpex = rows.filter(row => row.market === '上櫃').length;

      renderQuickList(rows);
    }
    function render() {
      syncModelLabels();
      const filtered = getFilteredRows();
      updateSummary(filtered);

      filtered.sort((a, b) => {
        const av = sortKey === 'strong' ? modelStrong(a)
          : sortKey === 'macdPositive' ? modelMacdPositive(a)
          : sortKey === 'model' ? modelLabel(a)
          : a[sortKey];
        const bv = sortKey === 'strong' ? modelStrong(b)
          : sortKey === 'macdPositive' ? modelMacdPositive(b)
          : sortKey === 'model' ? modelLabel(b)
          : b[sortKey];
        if (av === null || av === undefined) return bv === null || bv === undefined ? 0 : 1;
        if (bv === null || bv === undefined) return -1;
        let result;
        if (typeof av === 'number' && typeof bv === 'number') {
          result = av - bv;
        } else if (typeof av === 'boolean' && typeof bv === 'boolean') {
          result = Number(av) - Number(bv);
        } else {
          result = String(av).localeCompare(String(bv), 'zh-Hant', { numeric: true });
        }
        return sortDirection === 'asc' ? result : -result;
      });

      document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {

        th.classList.remove('asc', 'desc');
        if (th.dataset.sort === sortKey) th.classList.add(sortDirection);
      });

      countEl.textContent = `顯示 ${filtered.length} / ${DATA.length} 檔`;
      if (!filtered.length) {
        rowsEl.innerHTML = '<tr><td class="empty" colspan="15">沒有符合目前篩選條件的股票。</td></tr>';
        return;
      }

      const relativeStrength = value => {
        if (value === null || value === undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(value))) {
          return '<span class="rs-neutral">—</span>';
        }
        const number = Number(value);
        const className = number > 0 ? 'rs-positive' : number < 0 ? 'rs-negative' : 'rs-neutral';
        const prefix = number > 0 ? '+' : '';
        return `<span class="${className}">${prefix}${number.toFixed(2)}%</span>`;
      };

      const averageVolume = value => {
        if (value === null || value === undefined || Number.isNaN(Number(value))) return '—';
        return Math.round(Number(value)).toLocaleString('zh-TW');
      };

      rowsEl.innerHTML = filtered.map(row => `
        <tr>
          <td data-label="市場">${row.market}</td>
          <td data-label="代號"><strong>${row.code}</strong></td>
          <td data-label="名稱">${row.name}</td>
          <td data-label="KD日期">${row.kd}</td>
          <td data-label="MACD日期">${row.macd}</td>
          <td data-label="量能">${badge(row.volume)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="20日平均成交張數">${averageVolume(row.avgVolume20)}</td>
          <td data-label="KD3強勢">${modelStrong(row) ? '<span class="badge strong">Y</span>' : 'N'}</td>
          <td data-label="符合模型">${modelLabel(row)}</td>
          <td data-label="MACD > 0">${badge(modelMacdPositive(row))}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="5日相對強度">${relativeStrength(row.rs5)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="20日相對強度">${relativeStrength(row.rs20)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="收盤價">${Number(row.close).toFixed(2)}</td>
          <td class="num" data-label="連續天數">${row.streak}</td>
          <td data-label="日K"><button class="chart-button" type="button" data-code="${row.code}">日K</button></td>
          <td class="mobile-chart-cell"><div class="mobile-chart" data-code="${row.code}"></div></td>
        </tr>
      `).join('');
      rowsEl.querySelectorAll('.mobile-chart').forEach(el => {
        const row = DATA.find(item => item.code === el.dataset.code);
        if (row) el.innerHTML = chartSvgMarkup(row, true);
      });
    }

    controls.forEach(el => el.addEventListener('input', render));
    controls.forEach(el => el.addEventListener('change', render));
    reportDateEl.addEventListener('change', () => {
      selectedDate = reportDateEl.value;
      DATA = HISTORY[selectedDate] || [];
      chartPanel.hidden = true;
      render();
    });
    rowsEl.addEventListener('click', event => {
      const button = event.target.closest('.chart-button');
      if (!button) return;
      const row = DATA.find(item => item.code === button.dataset.code);
      if (row) renderChart(row);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('th.sortable').forEach(th => {
      th.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const key = th.dataset.sort;
        if (sortKey === key) {
          sortDirection = sortDirection === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
        } else {
          sortKey = key;
          sortDirection = ['close', 'streak', 'volume', 'avgVolume20', 'strong', 'macdPositive', 'rs5', 'rs20'].includes(key) ? 'desc' : 'asc';
        }
        render();
      });
    });
    render();
  </script>
</body>

          <h2>策略說明</h2>
          <ul>
            <li>KD：觀察短線動能，名單列出近期 KD 黃金交叉標的。</li>
            <li>MACD：可切換 MACD130 或 MACD60 篩選法，動能條件均使用最近 3 日 Hist 判斷。</li>
            <li>MACD > 0：目前所選模型的 MACD 訊號線為正；預設不作硬性排除，勾選後才篩選。</li>
            <li>量能：Y 表示 5 日均量大於 10 日均量。</li>
            <li>20日均量：最近 20 個交易資料日的平均成交張數；上市成交股數除以 1,000，上櫃成交仟股直接換算為張。篩選只影響畫面顯示，不改變原始選股名單。</li>
            <li>KD3強勢：近期 KD 黃金交叉後，K-D 差距持續擴大。</li>
            <li><strong>相對強度（RS）：個股同期報酬率減去所屬市場指數同期報酬率。</strong>5 日約代表一週，20 日約代表一個月；上市股票與加權指數比較，上櫃股票與櫃買指數比較。正值表示同期跑贏大盤，負值表示同期落後大盤，僅供排序比較，不代表未來漲跌。</li>
          </ul>

          <h2>策略說明</h2>
          <ul>
            <li>KD：觀察短線動能，名單列出近期 KD 黃金交叉標的。</li>
            <li>MACD：可切換 MACD130 或 MACD60 篩選法，動能條件均使用最近 3 日 Hist 判斷。</li>
            <li>MACD > 0：目前所選模型的 MACD 訊號線為正；預設不作硬性排除，勾選後才篩選。</li>
            <li>量能：Y 表示 5 日均量大於 10 日均量。</li>
            <li>20日均量：最近 20 個交易資料日的平均成交張數；上市成交股數除以 1,000，上櫃成交仟股直接換算為張。篩選只影響畫面顯示，不改變原始選股名單。</li>
            <li>KD3強勢：近期 KD 黃金交叉後，K-D 差距持續擴大。</li>
            <li><strong>相對強度（RS）：個股同期報酬率減去所屬市場指數同期報酬率。</strong>5 日約代表一週，20 日約代表一個月；上市股票與加權指數比較，上櫃股票與櫃買指數比較。正值表示同期跑贏大盤，負值表示同期落後大盤，僅供排序比較，不代表未來漲跌。</li>
          </ul>
        </div>

          <ul>
            <li>KD：觀察短線動能，名單列出近期 KD 黃金交叉標的。</li>
            <li>MACD：可切換 MACD130 或 MACD60 篩選法，動能條件均使用最近 3 日 Hist 判斷。</li>
            <li>MACD > 0：目前所選模型的 MACD 訊號線為正；預設不作硬性排除，勾選後才篩選。</li>
            <li>量能：Y 表示 5 日均量大於 10 日均量。</li>
            <li>20日均量：最近 20 個交易資料日的平均成交張數；上市成交股數除以 1,000，上櫃成交仟股直接換算為張。篩選只影響畫面顯示，不改變原始選股名單。</li>
            <li>KD3強勢：近期 KD 黃金交叉後，K-D 差距持續擴大。</li>
            <li><strong>相對強度（RS）：個股同期報酬率減去所屬市場指數同期報酬率。</strong>5 日約代表一週，20 日約代表一個月；上市股票與加權指數比較，上櫃股票與櫃買指數比較。正值表示同期跑贏大盤，負值表示同期落後大盤，僅供排序比較，不代表未來漲跌。</li>

          </ul>

          <h2>策略說明</h2>
          <ul>
            <li>KD：觀察短線動能，名單列出近期 KD 黃金交叉標的。</li>
            <li>MACD：可切換 MACD130 或 MACD60 篩選法，動能條件均使用最近 3 日 Hist 判斷。</li>
            <li>MACD > 0：目前所選模型的 MACD 訊號線為正；預設不作硬性排除，勾選後才篩選。</li>
            <li>量能：Y 表示 5 日均量大於 10 日均量。</li>
            <li>20日均量：最近 20 個交易資料日的平均成交張數；上市成交股數除以 1,000，上櫃成交仟股直接換算為張。篩選只影響畫面顯示，不改變原始選股名單。</li>
            <li>KD3強勢：近期 KD 黃金交叉後，K-D 差距持續擴大。</li>
            <li><strong>相對強度（RS）：個股同期報酬率減去所屬市場指數同期報酬率。</strong>5 日約代表一週，20 日約代表一個月；上市股票與加權指數比較，上櫃股票與櫃買指數比較。正值表示同期跑贏大盤，負值表示同期落後大盤，僅供排序比較，不代表未來漲跌。</li>
          </ul>

          <ul>

            <li>量能：Y 表示 5 日均量大於 10 日均量。</li>
            <li>20日均量：最近 20 個交易資料日的平均成交張數；上市成交股數除以 1,000，上櫃成交仟股直接換算為張。篩選只影響畫面顯示，不改變原始選股名單。</li>
            <li>KD3強勢：近期 KD 黃金交叉後，K-D 差距持續擴大。</li>
            <li><strong>相對強度（RS）：個股同期報酬率減去所屬市場指數同期報酬率。</strong>5 日約代表一週，20 日約代表一個月；上市股票與加權指數比較，上櫃股票與櫃買指數比較。正值表示同期跑贏大盤，負值表示同期落後大盤，僅供排序比較，不代表未來漲跌。</li>
          </ul>
